Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the cellular object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 161. The moduli space has dimension at most 200 with 1 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_161(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 161. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^200(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)
